HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000908/B005","root_000934/B001","root_001390/B001","root_001390/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_020cb25f079a663dfbde","text":"1. **Negation and exception close the provisioning system around one unusable input.** **Lexical:** `ل ي س` B001, fixed present-state negation, and B002, exception or exclusion; `ط ع م` B001, food as what is tasted and satisfies hunger; `ض ر ع` B005, the plant called `ضريع`. **Cue:** `لَّيْسَ ... إِلَّا` first denies the whole category and then admits a single source, which 88:7 immediately tests and finds non-fattening and non-availing. **Role:** `ليس` closes access, `إلا` acts as the narrow gate, `طعام` states the promised function, and `ضريع` fills the slot without fulfilling it. **Abduction:** I infer that 88:7 supplies a functional test of the noun in 88:6. **Reading change:** the ayah reads as a categorical trap: something can occupy the grammatical position of food while being excluded from food's bodily purpose.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000908/B005","root_000934/B001","root_001390/B001","root_001390/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0ef8f0e62a2eab2a3921","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:6:1\",\"root_001390\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]],[\"88:6:3\",\"root_000934\",[\"B001\"]],[\"88:6:6\",\"root_000908\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Olumsuzlama ve istisna, beslenme alanını tek bir girdiye kapatır; bu girdi yiyeceğin dilbilgisel yerini doldurur ama sonraki işlev sınamasında yiyecek olmayı başaramaz.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000908/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1f76cc459c2aa79ff0f1","text":"88:7 supplied the decisive functional test: the sole \"food\" neither fattens nor avails, activating both `ض ر ع` B003 emaciation and the anti-udder contrast with B001. 88:8-11 then showed that the exception is not scarcity alone but exclusion from an integrated regime of ease, satisfaction, shelter, and quiet.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000908/B002","root_000908/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_26724d4ace841d41063c","text":"3. **The food externalizes the eater's own abasement and wasting.** **Lexical:** `ض ر ع` B002, \"humble supplication and submission,\" and B003, \"weakness and emaciation.\" **Cue:** the plant follows `خَاشِعَةٌ` in 88:2 and `نَّاصِبَةٌ` in 88:3, then leads directly to hunger and failure to fatten in 88:7. **Role:** B002 mirrors the eater's lowered social-affective posture; B003 mirrors the body's depleted result. **Abduction:** I infer a material analogy in which consumed matter reproduces the consumer's condition. **Reading change:** the plant is no longer an arbitrary menu item; it is a bodily and social mechanism that feeds abasement back into the abased.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_309f3eec7bc7281fd3f3","text":"The later camel inspection and `نعم` branch for grazing livestock made the pastoral inversion much stronger than at first sight. The following `لا يسمن ولا يغني` confirmed that the exception is deliberately non-functional.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000908/B001","root_000934/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5610efcc3e15609d4daa","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:6:3\",\"root_000934\",[\"B007\"]],[\"88:6:6\",\"root_000908\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Bitki adının meme ve sütü de çağrıştırması, yağlanmayla ilişkilenen yiyecek fikriyle birlikte tersine çevrilmiş bir süt düzeni kurar: Besin yolu açılır gibi olur, fakat bedende hiçbir dolgunluk üretmez.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000908/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_71c4f16c84cb7154102f","text":"2. **Hunger as supplicatory weakness without mercy.** Evidence: `ض ر ع` B002 humble supplication/submission, B003 weakness/emaciation; opening `ر ح م` B001 mercy; later `ج و ع` B001 hunger. Structural cue: after forced drink, the only food-root carries submission and weakness. Role: `ضريع` embodies the eater’s own humbled need. Abductive move: deprivation can make the consumed object mirror the consumer’s state. Reading change: the food becomes an image of emaciated pleading, not simply a botanical item.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000908/B005","root_000934/B001","root_001390/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ca9bb50237c9dc6743a0","text":"1. **Food reduced to excluded, non-nourishing plant.** Evidence: `ل ي س` B001 present-state denial, B002 exception/exclusion; `ط ع م` B001 taste/food; `ض ر ع` B005 plant called dari. Structural cue: the ayah denies food and then excepts a single source. Role: `ليس` empties the food category; `إلا` leaves a residual plant-name. Abductive move: an exception can expose the failure of the category it seems to preserve. Reading change: the ayah reads as “food” stripped to a technical remnant that does not function as food.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000908/B001","root_000934/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e6b056c45e145544e516","text":"2. **The plant is an anti-udder: provision is named through a root of milk but yields no flesh.** **Lexical:** `ض ر ع` B001, \"udder and milk,\" behind the surface B005 plant branch; `ط ع م` B007, \"animal fatness and fatty taste,\" activated by `لَا يُسْمِنُ` in 88:7. **Cue:** a root capable of naming milk-bearing anatomy instead names the sole plant-food, and the next ayah explicitly denies fattening. **Role:** the udder branch supplies the expected nutritive pathway, while the plant branch occupies and reverses it. **Abduction:** I infer that root-sharing plus the immediate body test invites a lactational counter-image; the ayah does not mention milk. **Reading change:** `ضريع` becomes materially ironic: its root opens a route toward rich animal provision, but its actual role is to block that route.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000744/B001","root_000908/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ee249640857d0dc8b6d9","text":"3. **Anti-lactation pastoral image.** Evidence: `ض ر ع` B001 udder/milk, B005 plant; `س م ن` B001 fatness is negated in 88:7; later `ء ب ل` B001 camels and `ن ع م` B005 grazing livestock. Structural cue: the surah’s pastoral lexicon includes camels, livestock, fatness, hunger, grazing, and watering. Role: `ضريع` evokes the milk/udder system but gives no nourishing milk. Abductive move: a punishment scene can invert the pastoral chain of water, pasture, milk, fat, and satiety. Reading change: the ayah reads as failed animal husbandry: pasture without nourishment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000908/B002","root_000908/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_feb9376dd360a54a4f26","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:6:6\",\"root_000908\",[\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Bitkinin yalvaran alçalış ve zayıflığı da çağrıştırması, yiyenin kendi ezilmiş ve tükenmiş durumunu ona geri veren maddi bir aynaya dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
